- **skit-ux-copy**: Write, rewrite or check the words on buttons, labels, errors, empty states, onboarding and notifications. - **skit-vibe**: Build a screen or UI change, decide how much process it needs, and look at the rendered result before calling it done. It owns building UI. Not for non-UI work, or for reviewing or critiquing an existing UI (skit-interface); visual direction and type come from skit-visual-design and skit-type. ([sudakshsoti/skit](https://github.com/sudakshsoti/skit/tree/HEAD/skills/skit-vibe))
- **skit-visual-design**: Set the visual direction of a product UI or brand page: colour in OKLCH, hierarchy, responsive layout and CSS tokens, after first working out what kind of surface it is. Triggers: "make it look better", "this looks like every AI app", "it looks generic", "pick the colours", "dark mode looks wrong", "too many borders and shadows", "the page feels crowded", "design a landing page", "write the style guide". Not for a token or colour tweak inside an existing system, typography-primary work (skit-type), behaviour and states or UI review (skit-interface), or building the change (skit-vibe).
